- **porting-debugger**: Digs into one specific failure in a port until it finds the cause or exhausts the leads. Examples are a Lingo error ScummVM throws, a Wine crash, a black scene or a missing sound. It reads traces, engine source and bug trackers and runs focused experiments, preferring windowless ones, then returns a diagnosis with evidence and the next experiments. Use it when a failure needs deep reading that would flood the lead's context. ([callumgare/second-chance](https://github.com/callumgare/second-chance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/porting-debugger.md) · ★3)

- **poteto-code**: poteto-mode on the code model. Use for the playbooks that change code (feature, refactoring, bug fix, perf issue, hillclimb, prototype, visual parity) and for code-writing delegates inside a playbook. Verifies against a baseline instead of asserting. ([ericboehs/dotfiles](https://github.com/ericboehs/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.pi-agent/agents/poteto-code.md) · ★10)
- **poteto-code**: Code-writing delegate for poteto-mode playbook steps (feature, refactoring, bug fix, perf, hillclimb). Takes a tight scope with file paths, a named data shape, and success criteria, and returns a reviewed-ready diff. Use for mechanical and moderately hard changes. Route the hardest changes to `poteto-judge` instead. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-code.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-coder**: pstack's fast code delegate. Executes precisely specified implementation scopes (file paths, named data shapes, exact steps) to the letter. Used by the poteto-mode playbooks for feature, bug-fix, perf, hillclimb, and refactoring implementation. Not for vague intents or cross-cutting design. ([philrenaud/pstack-opencode](https://github.com/philrenaud/pstack-opencode/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/poteto-coder.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **poteto-explainer**: Synthesis and explanation delegate. Backs the `how` skill's explainer, the `why` skill's synthesizer, and `teach`. Takes explorer findings and weaves them into one cited explanation. Read-only, and keeps MCP access for spot-verifying citations. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-explainer.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-explorer**: Read-only exploration and investigation worker. Backs the `how` skill's explorers, the `why` skill's investigators, and `swarm` workers. Returns file pointers, conventions, entry points, and cited evidence. Never writes application code. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-explorer**: Read-only exploration, review, and auditing subagent (`EnableWriteTools: false`, `EnableMCPTools: false`, `EnableSubagentTools: false`). Use when spawning read-only explorers, reviewers, or judges from nested subagents where built-in `TypeName: "research"` is restricted, or whenever a strict read-only subagent is needed. ([matthew110703/pstack-rewire](https://github.com/matthew110703/pstack-rewire/blob/HEAD/antigravity/agents/poteto-explorer.md))
- **poteto-fable**: Third-family writing delegate. Exists so `arena` and `architect` can fan out across genuinely different models instead of the same model N times. Equivalent in standing to `poteto-code` and `poteto-judge`; pick it when a panel needs a family those two don't cover. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-fable.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-gpt**: pstack's second panel seat, pinned to Sonnet. The poteto-gpt name is the existing routing identifier. Used on interrogate, arena, and architect panels, plus reflect tooling. The spawning skill supplies the full prompt and rubric. ([philrenaud/pstack-opencode](https://github.com/philrenaud/pstack-opencode/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/poteto-gpt.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **poteto-grok**: pstack's fast exploration and third panel seat, pinned to Haiku. The poteto-grok name is the existing routing identifier. - **poteto-haiku**: `poteto-agent` pinned to the `haiku` model role. Use when a pstack skill names `haiku` for a `pstack:poteto-agent` subagent. ([MomePP/pstack-omp](https://github.com/MomePP/pstack-omp/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/poteto-haiku.md))

- **poteto-judge**: Highest-judgment delegate for poteto-mode. Use when a wrong call is expensive to unwind; route routine code changes to `poteto-code`. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-judge.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-judge**: Read-only judgment subagent for poteto-mode review, explanation, and synthesis seats (how explainer, why synthesizer, reflect reviewers, arena cross-judge, interrogate Reviewer A). - **poteto-judge**: pstack delegate for judgment, prose, and the hardest changes spawned during a `/skill:poteto-mode` run. Use where a pstack skill names a Claude model for a delegate or panel seat. Spawn a fresh one for each new task. Reads the `poteto-mode` skill in full before any work. ([zzzint/config](https://github.com/zzzint/config/blob/HEAD/build/pstack/omp/agents/poteto-judge.md) · 2 copies)
- **poteto-judgment**: poteto-mode on the judgment model. Use for architecture decisions, contested designs, prose and writing surfaces, product calls no experiment settles, and PR babysitting. ([ericboehs/dotfiles](https://github.com/ericboehs/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.pi-agent/agents/poteto-judgment.md) · ★10)
- **poteto-judgment**: pstack poteto-agent delegate for judgment, prose, and the hardest changes (cross-cutting design, gnarly concurrency, subtle algorithms). Spawn inside a poteto-mode playbook step, never to run the playbook itself. Spawn a fresh one per task. ([ChiThang-50Cent/ompstack](https://github.com/ChiThang-50Cent/ompstack/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-judgment.md) · ★2)
- **poteto-mode**: pstack's primary entry agent for non-trivial engineering work. Routes each task to the right playbook, applies the pstack principles, and demands evidence before calling anything done. Use for /poteto-mode, poteto-mode, or any investigation, bug fix, feature, refactor, perf, forensics, design, eval, PR, or unattended run. Stay out of the way for casual conversation and simple questions unless the user asks for rigor. - **poteto-mode**: Main-session coordinator that keeps poteto-mode on for every task, the Claude Code counterpart of starting poteto-mode as a Custom Mode. ([janozeli/pstack-port](https://github.com/janozeli/pstack-port/blob/HEAD/harness/pstack/claude-code/agents/poteto-mode.md))
- **poteto-mode**: poteto's agent style as a persistent mode. Every task in the session runs through the poteto-mode skill, its playbooks, and its principles.
